import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/app/bloc/app_bloc.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/app/view/app_view.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/di/service_locator.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/verification/presentation/cubit/verification_cubit.dart'; // <-- 1. Import

Future<void> main() async {
  WidgetsFlutterBinding.ensureInitialized();
  await configureDependencies();

  runApp(
    // 2. Use MultiBlocProvider to provide all global BLoCs/Cubits
    MultiBlocProvider(
      providers: [
        BlocProvider(
          create: (context) => getIt<AppBloc>()
            ..add(const AppEvent.checkAuthStatus()),
        ),
        BlocProvider(
          create: (context) => getIt<VerificationCubit>()
            ..fetchStatus(), // <-- 3. Create and fetch status immediately
        ),
      ],
      child: const AppView(), // 4. Your main app widget
    ),
  );
}

import 'package:bloc/bloc.dart';
import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/services/secure_storage_service.dart';

part 'app_bloc.freezed.dart';
part 'app_event.dart';
part 'app_state.dart';

@injectable
class AppBloc extends Bloc<AppEvent, AppState> {
  final SecureStorageService _storageService;

  AppBloc(this._storageService) : super(const AppState.initial()) {
    on<_CheckAuthStatus>(_onCheckAuthStatus);
    on<_LogoutRequested>(_onLogoutRequested);
  }

  /// Handles the [CheckAuthStatus] event.
  /// Reads the token from secure storage and emits [Authenticated] or
  /// [Unauthenticated] accordingly.
  Future<void> _onCheckAuthStatus(
    _CheckAuthStatus event,
    Emitter<AppState> emit,
  ) async {
    // You could emit a loading state here if needed
    // emit(const AppState.loading());
    try {
      final token = await _storageService.readToken();
      if (token != null) {
        emit(const AppState.authenticated());
      } else {
        emit(const AppState.unauthenticated());
      }
    } catch (e) {
      // In case storage fails, default to unauthenticated
      emit(const AppState.unauthenticated());
    }
  }

  /// Handles the [LogoutRequested] event.
  /// Deletes the token from storage and emits [Unauthenticated].
  Future<void> _onLogoutRequested(
    _LogoutRequested event,
    Emitter<AppState> emit,
  ) async {
    try {
      await _storageService.deleteToken();
    } catch (e) {
      // Handle potential storage error, though we still want to log out
      print('Error deleting token: $e');
    }
    emit(const AppState.unauthenticated());
  }
}

    TResult Function(_Unauthenticated value)? unauthenticated,
    required TResult orElse(),
  }) {
    if (unauthenticated != null) {
      return unauthenticated(this);
    }
    return orElse();
  }
}

abstract class _Unauthenticated implements AppState {
  const factory _Unauthenticated() = _$UnauthenticatedImpl;
}

  const factory AppState.initial() = _Initial;

  /// The state when a user is successfully authenticated (token exists).

import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/api/interceptors.dart';

@lazySingleton
class ApiClient {
  final Dio dio;

  ApiClient(AuthInterceptor authInterceptor)
      : dio = Dio(
          BaseOptions(
            // IMPORTANT: Change YOUR_IP to your computer's IP address
            // (e.g., 192.168.1.5) or 'localhost' if using web.
            baseUrl: 'http://YOUR_IP:8080/api', 
            connectTimeout: const Duration(seconds: 10),
            receiveTimeout: const Duration(seconds: 10),
            headers: {
              'Content-Type': 'application/json',
              'Accept': 'application/json',
            },
          ),
        ) {
    // Add the auth interceptor to Dio's interceptor list
    dio.interceptors.add(authInterceptor);

    // You can add other interceptors here, like a logger
    dio.interceptors.add(LogInterceptor(
      requestBody: true,
      responseBody: true,
    ));
  }
}

import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/app/bloc/app_bloc.dart'; // <-- 1. Import AppBloc
import 'package:sahbifit/core/di/service_locator.dart'; // <-- 2. Import getIt
import 'package:sahbifit/core/services/secure_storage_service.dart';

@lazySingleton
class AuthInterceptor extends Interceptor {
  final SecureStorageService _storageService;

  AuthInterceptor(this._storageService);

  @override
  Future<void> onRequest(
    RequestOptions options,
    RequestInterceptorHandler handler,
  ) async {
    // Read the token from storage
    final String? token = await _storageService.readToken();

    // Add the Authorization header if the token exists
    if (token != null) {
      options.headers['Authorization'] = 'Bearer $token';
    }

    return super.onRequest(options, handler);
  }

  @override
  void onError(DioException err, ErrorInterceptorHandler handler) {
    if (err.response?.statusCode == 401) {
      print('--- 401 Unauthorized: Dispatching LogoutRequested ---');

      // 3. THIS IS THE CHANGE
      // Use getIt to find the globally registered AppBloc
      // and add the logout event. The AppBloc will handle
      // deleting the token and updating the app's state.
      try {
        getIt<AppBloc>().add(const AppEvent.logoutRequested());
      } catch (e) {
        print('Failed to get AppBloc from getIt to dispatch logout: $e');
      }
    }
    return super.onError(err, handler);
  }
}

import 'package:dio/dio.dart';

class ServerException implements Exception {
  final String message;

  ServerException({required this.message});

  /// This factory constructor creates a [ServerException] from a [DioException].
  /// It parses the Dio error to find the most helpful message.
  factory ServerException.fromDio(DioException e) {
    String message = 'An unknown server error occurred.';

    // Try to get a specific error message from the backend response
    if (e.response != null && e.response?.data != null) {
      // Check if the backend sent a map with a 'message' key
      // e.g., { "message": "Phone number is already registered." }
      if (e.response?.data is Map) {
        message = e.response?.data['message'] ?? message;
      }
      // Check if the backend just sent a plain text error
      else if (e.response?.data is String) {
        final responseString = e.response!.data as String;
        if (responseString.isNotEmpty) {
          message = responseString;
        }
      }
    } 
    // Handle common Dio error types
    else if (e.type == DioExceptionType.connectionTimeout ||
        e.type == DioExceptionType.receiveTimeout ||
        e.type == DioExceptionType.sendTimeout) {
      message = 'Connection timed out. Please try again.';
    } else if (e.type == DioExceptionType.connectionError) {
      message = 'Network error. Please check your internet connection.';
    } else if (e.type == DioExceptionType.cancel) {
      message = 'Request was cancelled.';
    }

    return ServerException(message: message);
  }
}

/// Thrown if the user is not authorized (e.g., 401 error).
/// Our interceptor might catch this first, but it's good to have.
class UnauthorizedException implements Exception {
  final String message;
  UnauthorizedException({this.message = 'You are not authorized.'});
}

/// Thrown when dealing with local data storage.
class CacheException implements Exception {}

import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';

/// A [Failure] is an error that is safe to show to the user
/// and will be handled by the UI/Presentation layer.
abstract class Failure extends Equatable {
  final String message;

  const Failure({required this.message});

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [message];
}

/// Represents a failure from the server (e.g., 4xx, 5xx errors).
/// This is what we used in our [AuthRepositoryImpl].
class ServerFailure extends Failure {
  const ServerFailure({required super.message});
}

/// Represents a failure when accessing local cache.
class CacheFailure extends Failure {
  const CacheFailure({super.message = 'Error reading from local storage.'});
}

/// Represents a failure when there is no network connection.
class NetworkFailure extends Failure {
  const NetworkFailure({super.message = 'Please check your internet connection.'});
}

import 'package:flutter_secure_storage/flutter_secure_storage.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';

@lazySingleton
class SecureStorageService {
  final FlutterSecureStorage _storage;

  // Use const for keys
  static const String _keyToken = 'jwt_token';

  SecureStorageService(this._storage);

  Future<void> writeToken(String token) async {
    await _storage.write(key: _keyToken, value: token);
  }

  Future<String?> readToken() async {
    return await _storage.read(key: _keyToken);
}

  Future<void> deleteToken() async {
    await _storage.delete(key: _keyToken);
  }
}

// We need to tell GetIt how to create FlutterSecureStorage
@module
abstract class StorageModule {
  @lazySingleton
  FlutterSecureStorage get storage => const FlutterSecureStorage();
}

import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/failure.dart';

/// [Type] is the return type of the usecase (e.g., AuthToken, void).
/// [Params] is the parameters class (e.g., LoginParams).
abstract class UseCase<Type, Params> {
  /// The [call] method is the single entry point for the usecase.
  /// It's designed to be asynchronous and return either a [Failure]
  /// or the expected [Type].
  ///
  /// Note: In our Auth implementation, we are letting exceptions bubble up
  /// to the BLoC. You can also use an Either<Failure, Type> wrapper here.
  /// For simplicity, we'll stick to throwing Failures.
  Future<Type> call(Params params);
}

/// A helper class for usecases that do not require any parameters.
/// For example: `GetMyProfile(NoParams())`
class NoParams extends Equatable {
  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [];
}

import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/api/api_client.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/constants/api_endpoints.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/exceptions.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/data/models/auth_response_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/data/models/login_request_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/data/models/register_request_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/data/models/resend_otp_request_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/data/models/verify_sms_request_model.dart';

// This is the abstract interface
abstract class AuthRemoteDatasource {
  Future<AuthResponseModel> login(LoginRequestModel request);
  Future<void> register(RegisterRequestModel request);
  Future<void> verifySms(VerifySmsRequestModel request);
  Future<void> resendOtp(ResendOtpRequestModel request);
}

@Injectable(as: AuthRemoteDatasource)
class AuthRemoteDatasourceImpl implements AuthRemoteDatasource {
  final ApiClient _apiClient;

  AuthRemoteDatasourceImpl(this._apiClient);

  @override
  Future<AuthResponseModel> login(LoginRequestModel request) async {
    try {
      final response = await _apiClient.dio.post(
        ApiEndpoints.login, // <-- USE CONSTANT
        data: request.toJson(),
      );
      return AuthResponseModel.fromJson(response.data);
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw ServerException.fromDio(e);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> register(RegisterRequestModel request) async {
    try {
      await _apiClient.dio.post(
        ApiEndpoints.register, // <-- USE CONSTANT
        data: request.toJson(),
      );
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw ServerException.fromDio(e);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> verifySms(VerifySmsRequestModel request) async {
    try {
      await _apiClient.dio.post(
        ApiEndpoints.verifySms, // <-- USE CONSTANT
        data: request.toJson(),
      );
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw ServerException.fromDio(e);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> resendOtp(ResendOtpRequestModel request) async {
    try {
      await _apiClient.dio.post(
        ApiEndpoints.resendOtp, // <-- USE CONSTANT
        data: request.toJson(),
      );
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw ServerException.fromDio(e);
    }
  }
}

import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/exceptions.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/failure.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/services/secure_storage_service.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/data/datasources/auth_remote_datasource.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/data/models/login_request_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/data/models/register_request_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/data/models/resend_otp_request_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/data/models/verify_sms_request_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/domain/entities/auth_token.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/domain/repositories/auth_repository.dart';

@Injectable(as: AuthRepository)
class AuthRepositoryImpl implements AuthRepository {
  final AuthRemoteDatasource _remoteDatasource;
  final SecureStorageService _storageService;

  AuthRepositoryImpl(this._remoteDatasource, this._storageService);

  @override
  Future<AuthToken> login(String phoneNumber, String password) async {
    try {
      final requestModel = LoginRequestModel(
        phoneNumber: phoneNumber,
        password: password,
      );
      final responseModel = await _remoteDatasource.login(requestModel);

      // --- 
      // ðŸ’¡ KEY REQUIREMENT: Write the token to secure storage
      // ---
      await _storageService.writeToken(responseModel.jwtToken);
      
      return responseModel.toEntity();
    } on ServerException catch(e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> register(RegisterRequestModel request) async {
    try {
      await _remoteDatasource.register(request);
    } on ServerException catch(e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> verifySms(String phoneNumber, String otp) async {
    try {
      final requestModel = VerifySmsRequestModel(
        phoneNumber: phoneNumber,
        otp: otp,
      );
      await _remoteDatasource.verifySms(requestModel);
    } on ServerException catch(e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message);
    }
  }
    
  @override
  Future<void> resendOtp(String phoneNumber, String recaptchaToken) async {
    try {
      final requestModel = ResendOtpRequestModel(
        phoneNumber: phoneNumber,
        recaptchaToken: recaptchaToken,
      );
      await _remoteDatasource.resendOtp(requestModel);
    } on ServerException catch(e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message);
    }
  }
}

import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/usecases/usecase.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/data/models/register_request_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/domain/repositories/auth_repository.dart';

@injectable
class RegisterUser implements UseCase<void, RegisterParams> {
  final AuthRepository _repository;

  RegisterUser(this._repository);

  @override
  Future<void> call(RegisterParams params) async {
    // The Usecase maps the simple params into the data model
    final requestModel = RegisterRequestModel(
      phoneNumber: params.phoneNumber,
      password: params.password,
      recaptchaToken: params.recaptchaToken,
      firstName: params.firstName,
      dateOfBirth: params.dateOfBirth,
      primaryGymId: params.primaryGymId,
      goalTagIds: params.goalTagIds,
      scheduleTagIds: params.scheduleTagIds,
      profilePhotoUrl: params.profilePhotoUrl,
    );
    return _repository.register(requestModel);
  }
}

class RegisterParams extends Equatable {
  final String phoneNumber;
  final String password;
  final String recaptchaToken;
  final String firstName;
  final String dateOfBirth;
  final String primaryGymId;
  final List<int> goalTagIds;
  final List<int> scheduleTagIds;
  final String? profilePhotoUrl;

  const RegisterParams({
    required this.phoneNumber,
    required this.password,
    required this.recaptchaToken,
    required this.firstName,
    required this.dateOfBirth,
    required this.primaryGymId,
    required this.goalTagIds,
    required this.scheduleTagIds,
    this.profilePhotoUrl,
  });

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [
        phoneNumber,
        password,
        recaptchaToken,
        firstName,
        dateOfBirth,
        primaryGymId,
        goalTagIds,
        scheduleTagIds,
        profilePhotoUrl,
      ];
}

import 'package:bloc/bloc.dart';
import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';

import 'package:sahbifit/app/bloc/app_bloc.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/failure.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/domain/usecases/login_user.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/domain/usecases/register_user.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/domain/usecases/resend_otp.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/domain/usecases/verify_sms.dart';

part 'auth_bloc.freezed.dart';
part 'auth_event.dart';
part 'auth_state.dart';

@injectable
class AuthBloc extends Bloc<AuthEvent, AuthState> {
  final LoginUser _loginUser;
  final RegisterUser _registerUser;
  final VerifySms _verifySms;
  final ResendOtp _resendOtp;
  final AppBloc _appBloc; // Injected to dispatch events to the AppBloc

  AuthBloc(
    this._loginUser,
    this._registerUser,
    this._verifySms,
    this._resendOtp,
    this._appBloc,
  ) : super(const AuthState.initial()) {
    on<_LoginButtonPressed>(_onLoginButtonPressed);
    on<_RegisterButtonPressed>(_onRegisterButtonPressed);
    on<_VerifySmsButtonPressed>(_onVerifySmsButtonPressed);
    on<_ResendOtpButtonPressed>(_onResendOtpButtonPressed);
  }

  Future<void> _onLoginButtonPressed(
    _LoginButtonPressed event,
    Emitter<AuthState> emit,
  ) async {
    emit(const AuthState.loading());
    try {
      final params = LoginParams(
        phoneNumber: event.phoneNumber,
        password: event.password,
      );
      await _loginUser(params);

      // ---
      // ðŸš€ REQUIREMENT FULFILLED
      // On success, dispatch CheckAuthStatus to the AppBloc.
      // The AppBloc will read the new token and emit AppState.authenticated
      // ---
      _appBloc.add(const AppEvent.checkAuthStatus());

      emit(const AuthState.loginSuccess());
    } on ServerFailure catch (e) {
      emit(AuthState.failure(e.message));
    } catch (e) {
      emit(AuthState.failure(e.toString()));
    }
  }

  Future<void> _onRegisterButtonPressed(
    _RegisterButtonPressed event,
    Emitter<AuthState> emit,
  ) async {
    emit(const AuthState.loading());
    try {
      // The event already contains the complex RegisterParams
      await _registerUser(event.params);
      emit(AuthState.registrationSuccess(
        phoneNumber: event.params.phoneNumber,
      ));
    } on ServerFailure catch (e) {
      emit(AuthState.failure(e.message));
    } catch (e) {
      emit(AuthState.failure(e.toString()));
    }
  }

  Future<void> _onVerifySmsButtonPressed(
    _VerifySmsButtonPressed event,
    Emitter<AuthState> emit,
  ) async {
    emit(const AuthState.loading());
    try {
      final params = VerifySmsParams(
        phoneNumber: event.phoneNumber,
        otp: event.otp,
      );
      await _verifySms(params);
      emit(const AuthState.verificationSuccess());
    } on ServerFailure catch (e) {
      emit(AuthState.failure(e.message));
    } catch (e) {
      emit(AuthState.failure(e.toString()));
    }
  }

  Future<void> _onResendOtpButtonPressed(
    _ResendOtpButtonPressed event,
    Emitter<AuthState> emit,
  ) async {
    emit(const AuthState.loading());
    try {
      final params = ResendOtpParams(
        phoneNumber: event.phoneNumber,
        recaptchaToken: event.recaptchaToken,
      );
      await _resendOtp(params);
      emit(const AuthState.otpResent());
    } on ServerFailure catch (e) {
      emit(AuthState.failure(e.message));
    } catch (e) {
      emit(AuthState.failure(e.toString()));
    }
  }
}

import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/chat/data/models/message_model.dart';

abstract class ChatRemoteDatasource {
  Stream<List<MessageModel>> getChatStream(String sessionId);
  Future<void> sendMessage(String sessionId, MessageModel message);
}

@Injectable(as: ChatRemoteDatasource)
class ChatRemoteDatasourceImpl implements ChatRemoteDatasource {
  final FirebaseFirestore _firestore;

  ChatRemoteDatasourceImpl(this._firestore);

  // Your backend's FirebaseChatService.java creates docs in "session_chats"
  static const String _collection = 'session_chats';
  static const String _subCollection = 'messages';

  @override
  Stream<List<MessageModel>> getChatStream(String sessionId) {
    // 1. Get the collection of messages for the given session
    final collectionRef = _firestore
        .collection(_collection)
        .doc(sessionId)
        .collection(_subCollection)
        .orderBy('timestamp', descending: true); // Order by time

    // 2. Get a stream of snapshots
    return collectionRef.snapshots().map((querySnapshot) {
      // 3. For each snapshot, map the documents into MessageModels
      return querySnapshot.docs
          .map((doc) => MessageModel.fromSnapshot(doc))
          .toList();
    });
  }

  @override
  Future<void> sendMessage(String sessionId, MessageModel message) async {
    // 1. Get the reference to the "messages" subcollection
    final collectionRef = _firestore
        .collection(_collection)
        .doc(sessionId)
        .collection(_subCollection);

    // 2. Add the new message
    // We use .toFirestore() to convert the model to a map
    // and add the server-side timestamp.
    await collectionRef.add(message.toFirestore());
  }
}

import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/chat/domain/entities/message.dart'; // Ensure this path is correct

part 'message_model.freezed.dart';
part 'message_model.g.dart';

@freezed
class MessageModel with _$MessageModel {
  const MessageModel._();

  @JsonSerializable(
    fieldRename: FieldRename.snake, // Firestore often uses snake_case
    converters: [TimestampConverter()], // Use our custom converter
  )
  const factory MessageModel({
    @JsonKey(ignore: true) String? id, // We'll get the ID from the doc snapshot
    required String senderId,
    required String text,
    // When reading, 'timestamp' will be a Timestamp.
    // When writing, this will be null and replaced by toFirestore
    required DateTime? timestamp,
  }) = _MessageModel;

  /// Special factory for creating a model to *send* to Firestore
  /// This sets the timestamp to null, so we can use a server timestamp.
  factory MessageModel.forSending({
    required String senderId,
    required String text,
  }) =>
      MessageModel(
        senderId: senderId,
        text: text,
        timestamp: null, // Will be replaced by server timestamp
      );

  /// Factory for creating a model from a Firestore snapshot
  factory MessageModel.fromSnapshot(DocumentSnapshot doc) {
    final data = doc.data() as Map<String, dynamic>;
    // Use the generated fromJson factory, then add the doc ID
    return MessageModel.fromJson(data).copyWith(id: doc.id);
  }

  /// Factory for creating a model from a JSON map (required by freezed)
  /// This is the line that fixes the error.
  factory MessageModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) =>
      _$MessageModelFromJson(json);

  /// Converts this model into a Map to *write* to Firestore
  Map<String, dynamic> toFirestore() {
    // toJson() will now exist thanks to the .fromJson factory being added
    // and build_runner being run.
    final json = toJson();

    // Replace our null timestamp with the server's command
    json['timestamp'] = FieldValue.serverTimestamp();
    
    // Don't write the 'id' field back into the document data
    json.remove('id'); 
    
    return json;
  }

  // Converts the data-layer model to a domain-layer entity
  Message toEntity() {
    return Message(
      messageId: id ?? '', // Use the ID from the snapshot
      senderId: senderId,
      text: text,
      timestamp: timestamp ?? DateTime.now(), // Fallback if timestamp is null
    );
  }
}

// This is the custom converter for handling Firestore Timestamps
class TimestampConverter implements JsonConverter<DateTime?, Object> {
  const TimestampConverter();

  @override
  DateTime? fromJson(Object json) {
    if (json is Timestamp) {
      return json.toDate();
    }
    // Handle cases where it might already be null
    if (json == null) {
      return null;
    }
    // You could add more robust parsing here if needed, e.g., from String
    return null;
  }

  @override
  Object toJson(DateTime? time) {
    // If a time is provided, convert it to a Timestamp
    if (time != null) {
      return Timestamp.fromDate(time);
    }
    // If time is null, we intend to use a server timestamp.
    // The `toFirestore` method handles this explicitly, but setting
    // it here ensures consistency if toJson() were called directly.
    return FieldValue.serverTimestamp();
  }
}

import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/failure.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/chat/data/datasources/chat_remote_datasource.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/chat/data/models/message_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/chat/domain/entities/message.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/chat/domain/repositories/chat_repository.dart';

@Injectable(as: ChatRepository)
class ChatRepositoryImpl implements ChatRepository {
  final ChatRemoteDatasource _remoteDatasource;

  ChatRepositoryImpl(this._remoteDatasource);

  @override
  Stream<List<Message>> getChatStream(String sessionId) {
    // We catch errors on the stream itself
    return _remoteDatasource.getChatStream(sessionId).map((models) {
      return models.map((model) => model.toEntity()).toList();
    }).handleError((error) {
      // Log the error and bubble up a generic failure
      print('Firestore Stream Error: $error');
      throw ServerFailure(message: 'Could not load chat messages.');
    });
  }

  @override
  Future<void> sendMessage({
    required String sessionId,
    required String senderId,
    required String text,
  }) async {
    try {
      // Create the model to send
      final message = MessageModel.forSending(
        senderId: senderId,
        text: text,
      );
      await _remoteDatasource.sendMessage(sessionId, message);
    } on FirebaseException catch (e) {
      // Handle specific Firestore errors
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message ?? 'Failed to send message.');
    } catch (e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: 'An unknown error occurred.');
    }
  }
}

import 'dart:async'; // Required for StreamSubscription

import 'package:bloc/bloc.dart';
import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/failure.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/chat/domain/entities/message.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/chat/domain/usecases/get_chat_stream.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/chat/domain/usecases/send_message.dart';

part 'chat_cubit.freezed.dart';
part 'chat_state.dart';

@injectable
class ChatCubit extends Cubit<ChatState> {
  final GetChatStream _getChatStream;
  final SendMessage _sendMessage;

  // This subscription needs to be stored so we can cancel it later.
  StreamSubscription<List<Message>>? _messageSubscription;

  ChatCubit(
    this._getChatStream,
    this._sendMessage,
  ) : super(const ChatState.initial());

  /// Connects to the real-time message stream for a specific session.
  Future<void> listenToChat(String sessionId) async {
    // Cancel any previous subscription before starting a new one
    await _messageSubscription?.cancel();
    emit(const ChatState.loading());

    try {
      // 1. Call the use case to get the stream
      final messageStream = await _getChatStream(
        ChatStreamParams(sessionId: sessionId),
      );

      // 2. Listen to the stream
      _messageSubscription = messageStream.listen(
        (messages) {
          // 3. Every time new data comes in, emit a 'loaded' state
          emit(ChatState.loaded(messages: messages));
        },
        onError: (error) {
          // 4. Handle any errors that occur on the stream
          emit(ChatState.error(error.toString()));
        },
      );
    } on ServerFailure catch (e) {
      emit(ChatState.error(e.message));
    } catch (e) {
      emit(ChatState.error(e.toString()));
    }
  }

  /// Sends a new chat message.
  Future<void> sendMessage({
    required String sessionId,
    required String senderId, // This should be the ID of the logged-in user
    required String text,
  }) async {
    // Don't emit a loading state here.
    // The UI will update optimistically when the stream listener
    // receives the new message from Firestore.
    try {
      final params = SendMessageParams(
        sessionId: sessionId,
        senderId: senderId,
        text: text,
      );
      await _sendMessage(params);
      // On success, do nothing. The stream listener will update the state.
    } on ServerFailure catch (e) {
      // If sending fails, emit an error state
      emit(ChatState.error(e.message));
    } catch (e) {
      emit(ChatState.error(e.toString()));
    }
  }

  /// Overrides the [close] method to cancel the stream subscription
  /// when the Cubit is disposed. This is CRITICAL to prevent memory leaks.
  @override
  Future<void> close() {
    _messageSubscription?.cancel();
    return super.close();
  }
}

// coverage:ignore-file

part of 'chat_cubit.dart';

@freezed
class ChatState with _$ChatState {
  /// Chat has not been initialized or connected.
  const factory ChatState.initial() = _Initial;

  /// Connecting to the chat stream.
  const factory ChatState.loading() = _Loading;

  /// Successfully connected and listening for messages.
  const factory ChatState.loaded({required List<Message> messages}) = _Loaded;

  /// An error occurred (either connecting or sending a message).
  const factory ChatState.error(String message) = _Error;
}

import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/api/api_client.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/constants/api_endpoints.dart'; // <-- IMPORT
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/exceptions.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/gym/data/models/gym_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/gym/data/models/gym_submission_model.dart';

abstract class GymRemoteDatasource {
  /// Calls the GET /gyms endpoint
  Future<List<GymModel>> getApprovedGyms();

  /// Calls the POST /gyms/submit endpoint
  Future<void> submitGym(GymSubmissionModel request);
}

@Injectable(as: GymRemoteDatasource)
class GymRemoteDatasourceImpl implements GymRemoteDatasource {
  final ApiClient _apiClient;

  GymRemoteDatasourceImpl(this._apiClient);

  @override
  Future<List<GymModel>> getApprovedGyms() async {
    try {
      final response = await _apiClient.dio.get(ApiEndpoints.gyms); // <-- USE CONSTANT

      // The response.data is a List<dynamic> of JSON maps
      // We must convert it into a List<GymModel>
      return (response.data as List)
          .map((json) => GymModel.fromJson(json))
          .toList();
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw ServerException.fromDio(e);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> submitGym(GymSubmissionModel request) async {
    try {
      await _apiClient.dio.post(
        ApiEndpoints.submitGym, // <-- USE CONSTANT
        data: request.toJson(),
      );
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw ServerException.fromDio(e);
    }
  }
}

import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/gym/domain/entities/gym.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/gym/domain/entities/gym_status.dart';

part 'gym_model.freezed.dart';
part 'gym_model.g.dart';

@freezed
class GymModel with _$GymModel {
  const GymModel._(); // Private constructor for methods

  @JsonSerializable(fieldRename: FieldRename.none, explicitToJson: true) // <-- ADD THIS
  const factory GymModel({
    required String gymId,
    required String name,
    String? address,
    required GymStatus status,
    required int memberCount,
  }) = _GymModel;

  factory GymModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) =>
      _$GymModelFromJson(json);

  // Converts the data-layer model to a domain-layer entity
  Gym toEntity() {
    return Gym(
      gymId: gymId,
      name: name,
      address: address,
      status: status,
      memberCount: memberCount,
    );
  }
}

/// @nodoc
class _$GymModelCopyWithImpl<$Res, $Val extends GymModel>
    implements $GymModelCopyWith<$Res> {
  _$GymModelCopyWithImpl(this._value, this._then);

  // ignore: unused_field
  final $Val _value;
  // ignore: unused_field
  final $Res Function($Val) _then;

  /// Create a copy of GymModel
  /// with the given fields replaced by the non-null parameter values.
  @pragma('vm:prefer-inline')
  @override
  $Res call({
    Object? gymId = null,
    Object? name = null,
    Object? address = freezed,
    Object? status = null,
    Object? memberCount = null,
  }) {
    return _then(
      _value.copyWith(
            gymId: null == gymId
                ? _value.gymId
                : gymId // ignore: cast_nullable_to_non_nullable
                      as String,
            name: null == name
                ? _value.name
                : name // ignore: cast_nullable_to_non_nullable
                      as String,
            address: freezed == address
                ? _value.address
                : address // ignore: cast_nullable_to_non_nullable

import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/exceptions.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/failure.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/gym/data/datasources/gym_remote_datasource.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/gym/data/models/gym_submission_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/gym/domain/entities/gym.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/gym/domain/entities/gym_submission.dart'; 
import 'package:sahbifit/features/gym/domain/repositories/gym_repository.dart';

@Injectable(as: GymRepository)
class GymRepositoryImpl implements GymRepository {
  final GymRemoteDatasource _remoteDatasource;

  GymRepositoryImpl(this._remoteDatasource);

  @override
  Future<List<Gym>> getApprovedGyms() async {
    try {
      // 
      // ðŸ’¡ THIS IS THE CORRECTED LINE:
      //
      final gymModels = await _remoteDatasource.getApprovedGyms();
      //
      //
      return gymModels.map((model) => model.toEntity()).toList();
    } on ServerException catch (e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> submitGym(GymSubmission submission) async {
    try {
      final requestModel = GymSubmissionModel(
        name: submission.name,
        address: submission.address,
      );
      await _remoteDatasource.submitGym(requestModel);
    } on ServerException catch (e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message);
    }
  }
}

import 'package:bloc/bloc.dart';
import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/failure.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/usecases/usecase.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/gym/domain/entities/gym.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/gym/domain/usecases/get_approved_gyms.dart';

part 'gym_list_cubit.freezed.dart';
part 'gym_list_state.dart';

@injectable
class GymListCubit extends Cubit<GymListState> {
  final GetApprovedGyms _getApprovedGyms;

  GymListCubit(this._getApprovedGyms) : super(const GymListState.initial());

  /// Fetches the list of all approved gyms.
  Future<void> fetchApprovedGyms() async {
    emit(const GymListState.loading());
    try {
      final gyms = await _getApprovedGyms(NoParams());
      emit(GymListState.loaded(gyms: gyms));
    } on ServerFailure catch (e) {
      emit(GymListState.error(e.message));
    } catch (e) {
      emit(GymListState.error(e.toString()));
    }
  }
}

part of 'gym_list_cubit.dart';

@freezed
class GymListState with _$GymListState {
  /// The initial state, before gyms are fetched.
  const factory GymListState.initial() = _Initial;

  /// The state while fetching the list of gyms.
  const factory GymListState.loading() = _Loading;

  /// The state when the list of approved gyms is successfully loaded.
  const factory GymListState.loaded({required List<Gym> gyms}) = _Loaded;

  /// The state when an error occurs.
  const factory GymListState.error(String message) = _Error;
}

import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/api/api_client.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/constants/api_endpoints.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/exceptions.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/rating/data/models/create_rating_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/rating/data/models/pending_rating_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/rating/data/models/rating_model.dart';

abstract class RatingRemoteDatasource {
  Future<List<PendingRatingModel>> getPendingRatings();
  Future<RatingModel> createRating(CreateRatingModel request);
  Future<List<RatingModel>> getRatingsForUser(String userId);
}

@Injectable(as: RatingRemoteDatasource)
class RatingRemoteDatasourceImpl implements RatingRemoteDatasource {
  final ApiClient _apiClient;

  RatingRemoteDatasourceImpl(this._apiClient);

  @override
  Future<List<PendingRatingModel>> getPendingRatings() async {
    try {
      final response = await _apiClient.dio.get(ApiEndpoints.pendingRatings);
      return (response.data as List)
          .map((json) => PendingRatingModel.fromJson(json))
          .toList();
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw ServerException.fromDio(e);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<RatingModel> createRating(CreateRatingModel request) async {
    try {
      final response = await _apiClient.dio.post(
        ApiEndpoints.ratings,
        data: request.toJson(),
      );
      // Backend returns the full Rating object upon creation
      return RatingModel.fromJson(response.data);
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw ServerException.fromDio(e);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<List<RatingModel>> getRatingsForUser(String userId) async {
    try {
      final response = await _apiClient.dio.get(
        ApiEndpoints.ratingsForUser(userId),
      );
      return (response.data as List)
          .map((json) => RatingModel.fromJson(json))
          .toList();
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw ServerException.fromDio(e);
    }
  }
}

import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/rating/domain/entities/rating.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/data/models/user_model.dart';

part 'rating_model.freezed.dart';
part 'rating_model.g.dart';

// Helper model to represent the nested 'session' object
// We only extract the ID, as seen in the Rating entity
@freezed
class RatingSessionModel with _$RatingSessionModel {
  @JsonSerializable(fieldRename: FieldRename.none, explicitToJson: true) // <-- ADD THIS
  const factory RatingSessionModel({
    required String sessionId,
  }) = _RatingSessionModel;

  factory RatingSessionModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) =>
      _$RatingSessionModelFromJson(json);
}


@freezed
class RatingModel with _$RatingModel {
  const RatingModel._();

  @JsonSerializable(fieldRename: FieldRename.none, explicitToJson: true) // <-- ADD THIS
  const factory RatingModel({
    required String ratingId,
    required RatingSessionModel session, // Nested session object
    required UserModel ratingBy,
    required UserModel ratingFor,
    required int ratingValue,
    String? comment,
    required String createdAt, // Receive as ISO 8601 String
  }) = _RatingModel;

  factory RatingModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) =>
      _$RatingModelFromJson(json);

  Rating toEntity() {
    return Rating(
      ratingId: ratingId,
      sessionId: session.sessionId, // Extract the ID
      ratingBy: ratingBy.toEntity(),
      ratingFor: ratingFor.toEntity(),
      ratingValue: ratingValue,
      comment: comment,
      createdAt: DateTime.parse(createdAt), // Convert String to DateTime
    );
  }
}

import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/exceptions.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/failure.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/rating/data/datasources/rating_remote_datasource.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/rating/data/models/create_rating_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/rating/domain/entities/pending_rating.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/rating/domain/entities/rating.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/rating/domain/repositories/rating_repository.dart';

@Injectable(as: RatingRepository)
class RatingRepositoryImpl implements RatingRepository {
  final RatingRemoteDatasource _remoteDatasource;

  RatingRepositoryImpl(this._remoteDatasource);

  @override
  Future<List<PendingRating>> getPendingRatings() async {
    try {
      final models = await _remoteDatasource.getPendingRatings();
      return models.map((model) => model.toEntity()).toList();
    } on ServerException catch (e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message);
    }
  }
  
  @override
  Future<Rating> createRating(CreateRatingModel request) async {
    try {
      final model = await _remoteDatasource.createRating(request);
      return model.toEntity();
    } on ServerException catch (e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<List<Rating>> getRatingsForUser(String userId) async {
     try {
      final models = await _remoteDatasource.getRatingsForUser(userId);
      return models.map((model) => model.toEntity()).toList();
    } on ServerException catch (e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message);
    }
  }
}

import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/usecases/usecase.dart';

import 'package:sahbifit/features/rating/domain/entities/rating.dart';

@injectable
class CreateRating implements UseCase<Rating, CreateRatingParams> {
  final RatingRepository _repository;

  CreateRating(this._repository);

  @override
  Future<Rating> call(CreateRatingParams params) async {
    // Map from clean params to the data model
    final requestModel = CreateRatingModel(
      sessionId: params.sessionId,
      ratedUserId: params.ratedUserId,
      ratingValue: params.ratingValue,
      comment: params.comment,
    );
    return _repository.createRating(requestModel);
  }
}

  final String ratedUserId;
  final int ratingValue;
  final String? comment;

    required this.ratedUserId,
    required this.ratingValue,
    this.comment,
  });

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [sessionId, ratedUserId, ratingValue, comment];
}

import 'package:bloc/bloc.dart';
import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/failure.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/usecases/usecase.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/rating/domain/entities/pending_rating.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/rating/domain/usecases/create_rating.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/rating/domain/usecases/get_pending_ratings.dart';

part 'pending_rating_bloc.freezed.dart';
part 'pending_rating_event.dart';
part 'pending_rating_state.dart';

@injectable
class PendingRatingBloc extends Bloc<PendingRatingEvent, PendingRatingState> {
  final GetPendingRatings _getPendingRatings;
  final CreateRating _createRating;

  PendingRatingBloc(
    this._getPendingRatings,
    this._createRating,
  ) : super(const PendingRatingState.initial()) {
    on<_FetchList>(_onFetchList);
    on<_SubmitRating>(_onSubmitRating);
  }

  Future<void> _onFetchList(
    _FetchList event,
    Emitter<PendingRatingState> emit,
  ) async {
    emit(const PendingRatingState.loading());
    try {
      final ratings = await _getPendingRatings(NoParams());
      emit(PendingRatingState.loaded(pendingRatings: ratings));
    } on ServerFailure catch (e) {
      emit(PendingRatingState.error(e.message));
    } catch (e) {
      emit(PendingRatingState.error(e.toString()));
    }
  }

  Future<void> _onSubmitRating(
    _SubmitRating event,
    Emitter<PendingRatingState> emit,
  ) async {
    // Keep the old state's list while loading
    // Or just emit a generic loading state
    emit(const PendingRatingState.loading());

    try {
      final params = CreateRatingParams(
        sessionId: event.sessionId,
        ratedUserId: event.ratedUserId,
        ratingValue: event.ratingValue,
        comment: event.comment,
      );
      await _createRating(params);

      // Emit success and then immediately refresh the list
      emit(const PendingRatingState.submissionSuccess());
      add(const PendingRatingEvent.fetchList());
    } on ServerFailure catch (e) {
      emit(PendingRatingState.error(e.message));
    } catch (e) {
      emit(PendingRatingState.error(e.toString()));
    }
  }
}

@freezed
class PendingRatingEvent with _$PendingRatingEvent {
  /// Dispatched to load the initial list of pending ratings.
  const factory PendingRatingEvent.fetchList() = _FetchList;

  /// Dispatched when the user submits a rating for a specific user/session.
  const factory PendingRatingEvent.submitRating({
    required String sessionId,
    required String ratedUserId,
    required int ratingValue,
    String? comment,
  }) = _SubmitRating;

@freezed
class PendingRatingState with _$PendingRatingState {
  /// The initial state, before anything is loaded.
  const factory PendingRatingState.initial() = _Initial;

  /// The state while fetching the list or submitting a rating.
  const factory PendingRatingState.loading() = _Loading;

  /// The state when the list of pending ratings is successfully loaded.
  const factory PendingRatingState.loaded({
    required List<PendingRating> pendingRatings,
  }) = _Loaded;

  /// A temporary state to indicate one rating was submitted successfully.
  /// The BLoC will likely transition from this back to 'loaded' with an updated list.
  const factory PendingRatingState.submissionSuccess() = _SubmissionSuccess;

  /// The state when an error occurs.
  const factory PendingRatingState.error(String message) = _Error;
}

import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/api/api_client.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/constants/api_endpoints.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/exceptions.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/report/data/models/create_report_model.dart';

abstract class ReportRemoteDatasource {
  Future<void> createReport(CreateReportModel request);
}

@Injectable(as: ReportRemoteDatasource)
class ReportRemoteDatasourceImpl implements ReportRemoteDatasource {
  final ApiClient _apiClient;

  ReportRemoteDatasourceImpl(this._apiClient);

  @override
  Future<void> createReport(CreateReportModel request) async {
    try {
      await _apiClient.dio.post(
        ApiEndpoints.reports,
        data: request.toJson(),
      );
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw ServerException.fromDio(e);
    }
  }
}

import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/exceptions.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/failure.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/report/data/datasources/report_remote_datasource.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/report/data/models/create_report_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/report/domain/repositories/report_repository.dart';

@Injectable(as: ReportRepository)
class ReportRepositoryImpl implements ReportRepository {
  final ReportRemoteDatasource _remoteDatasource;

  ReportRepositoryImpl(this._remoteDatasource);

  @override
  Future<void> createReport({
    required String reportedUserId,
    required String reason,
    String? sessionId,
  }) async {
    try {
      final requestModel = CreateReportModel(
        reportedUserId: reportedUserId,
        reason: reason,
        sessionId: sessionId,
      );
      await _remoteDatasource.createReport(requestModel);
    } on ServerException catch (e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message);
    }
  }
}

import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/usecases/usecase.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/report/domain/repositories/report_repository.dart';

@injectable
class CreateReport implements UseCase<void, CreateReportParams> {
  final ReportRepository _repository;

  CreateReport(this._repository);

  @override
  Future<void> call(CreateReportParams params) async {
    return _repository.createReport(
      reportedUserId: params.reportedUserId,
      reason: params.reason,
      sessionId: params.sessionId,
    );
  }
}

class CreateReportParams extends Equatable {
  final String reportedUserId;
  final String reason;
  final String? sessionId;

  const CreateReportParams({
    required this.reportedUserId,
    required this.reason,
    this.sessionId,
  });

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [reportedUserId, reason, sessionId];
}

import 'package:bloc/bloc.dart';
import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/failure.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/report/domain/usecases/create_report.dart';

part 'report_cubit.freezed.dart';
part 'report_state.dart';

@injectable
class ReportCubit extends Cubit<ReportState> {
  final CreateReport _createReport;

  ReportCubit(this._createReport) : super(const ReportState.initial());

  /// Submits a user report to the server.
  Future<void> submitReport({
    required String reportedUserId,
    required String reason,
    String? sessionId,
  }) async {
    emit(const ReportState.loading());
    try {
      final params = CreateReportParams(
        reportedUserId: reportedUserId,
        reason: reason,
        sessionId: sessionId,
      );
      await _createReport(params);
      emit(const ReportState.success());
    } on ServerFailure catch (e) {
      emit(ReportState.error(e.message));
    } catch (e) {
      emit(ReportState.error(e.toString()));
    }
  }
}

import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';

import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/api/api_client.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/constants/api_endpoints.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/exceptions.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/data/datasources/session_remote_datasource.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/data/models/create_session_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/data/models/mark_attendance_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/data/models/session_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/verification/domain/enums/verified_gender_enum.dart';

@Injectable(as: SessionRemoteDatasource)
class SessionRemoteDatasourceImpl implements SessionRemoteDatasource {
  final ApiClient _apiClient;

  SessionRemoteDatasourceImpl(this._apiClient);

  @override
  Future<List<SessionModel>> getActiveSessions(VerifiedGender? genderFilter) async {
    try {
      final Map<String, dynamic> queryParams = {};
      if (genderFilter != null) {
        queryParams['genderFilter'] = genderFilter.name;
      }

      final response = await _apiClient.dio.get(
        ApiEndpoints.sessions,
        queryParameters: queryParams.isNotEmpty ? queryParams : null,
      );

      return (response.data as List)
          .map((json) => SessionModel.fromJson(json))
          .toList();
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw ServerException.fromDio(e);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<SessionModel> createSession(CreateSessionModel request) async {
    try {
      final response = await _apiClient.dio.post(
        ApiEndpoints.createSession,
        data: request.toJson(),
      );
      // Backend returns the created session
      return SessionModel.fromJson(response.data);
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw ServerException.fromDio(e);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> joinSession(String sessionId) async {
    try {
      await _apiClient.dio.post(
        ApiEndpoints.joinSession(sessionId), // Use function for dynamic URL
      );
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw ServerException.fromDio(e);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> markAttendance(MarkAttendanceModel request) async {
    try {
      await _apiClient.dio.put(
        ApiEndpoints.markAttendance,
        data: request.toJson(),
      );
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw ServerException.fromDio(e);
    }
  }
}

@freezed
class SessionModel with _$SessionModel {
  const SessionModel._(); // Private constructor for methods

  @JsonSerializable(fieldRename: FieldRename.none, explicitToJson: true) // <-- ADD THIS
  const factory SessionModel({
    required String sessionId,
    required String title,
    required SessionType sessionType,
    required SessionStatus status,
    required String startTime, // Receive as ISO 8601 String
    required String endTime,   // Receive as ISO 8601 String
    required int maxParticipants,
    required int participantCount,
    required UserModel creator, // Nested JSON object
    required GymModel gym,       // Nested JSON object
  }) = _SessionModel;

  factory SessionModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) =>
      _$SessionModelFromJson(json);

  // Converts the data-layer model to a domain-layer entity
  Session toEntity() {
    return Session(
      sessionId: sessionId,
      title: title,
      sessionType: sessionType,
      status: status,
      startTime: DateTime.parse(startTime), // Convert String to DateTime
      endTime: DateTime.parse(endTime),     // Convert String to DateTime
      maxParticipants: maxParticipants,
      participantCount: participantCount,
      creator: creator.toEntity(), // Convert nested model to entity
      gym: gym.toEntity(),         // Convert nested model to entity
    );
  }
}

import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/exceptions.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/failure.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/data/datasources/session_remote_datasource.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/data/models/create_session_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/data/models/mark_attendance_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/domain/entities/session.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/domain/enums/participant_status.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/domain/repositories/session_repository.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/verification/domain/enums/verified_gender_enum.dart';

@Injectable(as: SessionRepository)
class SessionRepositoryImpl implements SessionRepository {
  final SessionRemoteDatasource _remoteDatasource;

  SessionRepositoryImpl(this._remoteDatasource);

  @override
  Future<List<Session>> getActiveSessions(VerifiedGender? genderFilter) async {
    try {
      final sessionModels = await _remoteDatasource.getActiveSessions(genderFilter);
      return sessionModels.map((model) => model.toEntity()).toList();
    } on ServerException catch (e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<Session> createSession(CreateSessionModel request) async {
    try {
      final sessionModel = await _remoteDatasource.createSession(request);
      return sessionModel.toEntity();
    } on ServerException catch (e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> joinSession(String sessionId) async {
    try {
      await _remoteDatasource.joinSession(sessionId);
    } on ServerException catch (e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> markAttendance(String sessionId, String userId, ParticipantStatus status) async {
    try {
      final requestModel = MarkAttendanceModel(
        sessionId: sessionId,
        participantUserId: userId,
        status: status,
      );
      await _remoteDatasource.markAttendance(requestModel);
    } on ServerException catch (e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message);
    }
  }
}

import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/gym/domain/entities/gym.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/domain/enums/session_status.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/domain/enums/session_type.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/domain/entities/user.dart';

class Session extends Equatable {
  final String sessionId;
  final String title;
  final SessionType sessionType;
  final SessionStatus status;
  final DateTime startTime;
  final DateTime endTime;
  final int maxParticipants;
  final int participantCount; // From the @Formula
  final User creator; // Nested User entity
  final Gym gym;     // Nested Gym entity

  const Session({
    required this.sessionId,
    required this.title,
    required this.sessionType,
    required this.status,
    required this.startTime,
    required this.endTime,
    required this.maxParticipants,
    required this.participantCount,
    required this.creator,
    required this.gym,
  });

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [
        sessionId,
        title,
        sessionType,
        status,
        startTime,
        endTime,
        participantCount,
        creator,
        gym,
      ];
}

import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/usecases/usecase.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/data/models/create_session_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/domain/entities/session.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/domain/enums/session_type.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/domain/repositories/session_repository.dart';

@injectable
class CreateSession implements UseCase<Session, CreateSessionParams> {
  final SessionRepository _repository;

  CreateSession(this._repository);

  @override
  Future<Session> call(CreateSessionParams params) async {
    // Map from clean params to the data model
    final requestModel = CreateSessionModel(
      gymId: params.gymId,
      sessionType: params.sessionType,
      title: params.title,
      startTime: params.startTime.toIso8601String(), // Convert DateTime to String
      endTime: params.endTime.toIso8601String(),     // Convert DateTime to String
      maxParticipants: params.maxParticipants,
    );
    return _repository.createSession(requestModel);
  }
}

class CreateSessionParams extends Equatable {
  final String gymId;
  final SessionType sessionType;
  final String title;
  final DateTime startTime;
  final DateTime endTime;
  final int maxParticipants;

  const CreateSessionParams({
    required this.gymId,
    required this.sessionType,
    required this.title,
    required this.startTime,
    required this.endTime,
    required this.maxParticipants,
  });

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [
        gymId,
        sessionType,
        title,
        startTime,
        endTime,
        maxParticipants,
      ];
}

import 'package:sahbifit/features/verification/domain/enums/verified_gender_enum.dart';

@injectable
class GetActiveSessions implements UseCase<List<Session>, GetActiveSessionsParams> {
  final SessionRepository _repository;

  GetActiveSessions(this._repository);

  @override
  Future<List<Session>> call(GetActiveSessionsParams params) async {
    return _repository.getActiveSessions(params.genderFilter);
  }
}

class GetActiveSessionsParams extends Equatable {
  final VerifiedGender? genderFilter;

  const GetActiveSessionsParams({this.genderFilter});

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [genderFilter];
}

@injectable
class MarkAttendance implements UseCase<void, MarkAttendanceParams> {
  final SessionRepository _repository;

  MarkAttendance(this._repository);

  @override
  Future<void> call(MarkAttendanceParams params) async {
    return _repository.markAttendance(
      params.sessionId,
      params.userId,
      params.status,
    );
  }
}

import 'package:bloc/bloc.dart';
import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/failure.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/domain/entities/session.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/domain/usecases/get_active_sessions.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/verification/domain/enums/verified_gender_enum.dart';

part 'session_feed_cubit.freezed.dart';
part 'session_feed_state.dart';

@injectable
class SessionFeedCubit extends Cubit<SessionFeedState> {
  final GetActiveSessions _getActiveSessions;

  SessionFeedCubit(this._getActiveSessions)
      : super(const SessionFeedState.initial());

  /// Fetches the list of active sessions, optionally applying a gender filter.
  Future<void> fetchSessions({VerifiedGender? genderFilter}) async {
    emit(const SessionFeedState.loading());
    try {
      final params = GetActiveSessionsParams(genderFilter: genderFilter);
      final sessions = await _getActiveSessions(params);

      emit(SessionFeedState.loaded(
        sessions: sessions,
        genderFilter: genderFilter,
      ));
    } on ServerFailure catch (e) {
      emit(SessionFeedState.error(e.message));
    } catch (e) {
      emit(SessionFeedState.error(e.toString()));
    }
  }
}

part of 'session_feed_cubit.dart';

@freezed
class SessionFeedState with _$SessionFeedState {
  /// The initial state, before any sessions are fetched.
  const factory SessionFeedState.initial() = _Initial;

  /// The state while sessions are being fetched from the API.
  const factory SessionFeedState.loading() = _Loading;

  /// The state when sessions have been successfully loaded.
  const factory SessionFeedState.loaded({
    required List<Session> sessions,
    VerifiedGender? genderFilter, // Store the filter that was used
  }) = _Loaded;

  /// The state when an error occurs during fetching.
  const factory SessionFeedState.error(String message) = _Error;
}

import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/api/api_client.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/constants/api_endpoints.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/exceptions.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/tag/data/models/tag_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/tag/domain/entities/tag_type.dart';

abstract class TagRemoteDatasource {
  Future<List<TagModel>> getTagsByType(TagType type);
}

@Injectable(as: TagRemoteDatasource)
class TagRemoteDatasourceImpl implements TagRemoteDatasource {
  final ApiClient _apiClient;

  TagRemoteDatasourceImpl(this._apiClient);

  @override
  Future<List<TagModel>> getTagsByType(TagType type) async {
    try {
      final response = await _apiClient.dio.get(
        ApiEndpoints.tags,
        queryParameters: {
          'type': type.name, // Sends ?type=goal or ?type=schedule
        },
      );

      // Convert the List<dynamic> from JSON to List<TagModel>
      return (response.data as List)
          .map((json) => TagModel.fromJson(json))
          .toList();
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw ServerException.fromDio(e);
    }
  }
}

import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/tag/domain/entities/tag.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/tag/domain/entities/tag_type.dart';

part 'tag_model.freezed.dart';
part 'tag_model.g.dart';

@freezed
class TagModel with _$TagModel {
  const TagModel._(); // Private constructor for methods

  @JsonSerializable(fieldRename: FieldRename.none, explicitToJson: true) // <-- ADD THIS
  const factory TagModel({
    required int tagId,
    required String tagName,
    required TagType tagType,
  }) = _TagModel;

  factory TagModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) =>
      _$TagModelFromJson(json);

  // Converts the data-layer model to a domain-layer entity
  Tag toEntity() {
    return Tag(
      tagId: tagId,
      tagName: tagName,
      tagType: tagType,
    );
  }
}

import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/exceptions.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/failure.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/tag/data/datasources/tag_remote_datasource.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/tag/domain/entities/tag.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/tag/domain/entities/tag_type.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/tag/domain/repositories/tag_repository.dart';

@Injectable(as: TagRepository)
class TagRepositoryImpl implements TagRepository {
  final TagRemoteDatasource _remoteDatasource;

  TagRepositoryImpl(this._remoteDatasource);

  @override
  Future<List<Tag>> getTagsByType(TagType type) async {
    try {
      final tagModels = await _remoteDatasource.getTagsByType(type);
      // Map List<TagModel> to List<Tag>
      return tagModels.map((model) => model.toEntity()).toList();
    } on ServerException catch (e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message);
    }
  }
}

import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/usecases/usecase.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/tag/domain/entities/tag.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/tag/domain/entities/tag_type.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/tag/domain/repositories/tag_repository.dart';

@injectable
class GetTagsByType implements UseCase<List<Tag>, GetTagsByTypeParams> {
  final TagRepository _repository;

  GetTagsByType(this._repository);

  @override
  Future<List<Tag>> call(GetTagsByTypeParams params) async {
    return _repository.getTagsByType(params.type);
  }
}

class GetTagsByTypeParams extends Equatable {
  final TagType type;

  const GetTagsByTypeParams({required this.type});

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [type];
}

FILE: C:\Users\Ahmed\Desktop\sahbifit\mobile\lib\features\tag\presentation\cubit\tags_cubit.dart

import 'package:bloc/bloc.dart';
import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/failure.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/tag/domain/entities/tag.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/tag/domain/entities/tag_type.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/tag/domain/usecases/get_tags_by_type.dart';

part 'tags_cubit.freezed.dart';
part 'tags_state.dart';

@injectable
class TagsCubit extends Cubit<TagsState> {
  final GetTagsByType _getTagsByType;

  TagsCubit(this._getTagsByType) : super(const TagsState.initial());

  /// Fetches both GOAL and SCHEDULE tags concurrently.
  Future<void> loadAllTags() async {
    emit(const TagsState.loading());
    try {
      // Use Future.wait to fetch both lists in parallel
      final results = await Future.wait([
        _getTagsByType(const GetTagsByTypeParams(type: TagType.goal)),
        _getTagsByType(const GetTagsByTypeParams(type: TagType.schedule)),
      ]);

      // Extract the results
      final goalTags = results[0];
      final scheduleTags = results[1];

      emit(TagsState.loaded(
        goalTags: goalTags,
        scheduleTags: scheduleTags,
      ));
    } on ServerFailure catch (e) {
      emit(TagsState.error(e.message));
    } catch (e) {
      emit(TagsState.error(e.toString()));
    }
  }
}

part of 'tags_cubit.dart';

@freezed
class TagsState with _$TagsState {
  /// The initial state, before tags are fetched.
  const factory TagsState.initial() = _Initial;

  /// The state while fetching the tags from the API.
  const factory TagsState.loading() = _Loading;

  /// The state when both 'goal' and 'schedule' tags are successfully loaded.
  const factory TagsState.loaded({
    required List<Tag> goalTags,
    required List<Tag> scheduleTags,
  }) = _Loaded;

  /// The state when an error occurs during fetching.
  const factory TagsState.error(String message) = _Error;
}

import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/api/api_client.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/constants/api_endpoints.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/exceptions.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/data/models/update_fcm_token_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/data/models/update_visibility_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/data/models/user_model.dart';

abstract class UserRemoteDatasource {
  Future<UserModel> getMyProfile();
  Future<UserModel> getUserProfile(String userId);
  Future<void> blockUser(String userId);
  Future<void> updateFcmToken(UpdateFcmTokenModel request);
  Future<void> updateProfileVisibility(UpdateVisibilityModel request);
}

@Injectable(as: UserRemoteDatasource)
class UserRemoteDatasourceImpl implements UserRemoteDatasource {
  final ApiClient _apiClient;

  UserRemoteDatasourceImpl(this._apiClient);

  @override
  Future<UserModel> getMyProfile() async {
    try {
      final response = await _apiClient.dio.get(ApiEndpoints.myProfile);
      return UserModel.fromJson(response.data);
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw ServerException.fromDio(e);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<UserModel> getUserProfile(String userId) async {
    try {
      final response = await _apiClient.dio.get(
        ApiEndpoints.getUserProfile(userId),
      );
      return UserModel.fromJson(response.data);
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw ServerException.fromDio(e);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> blockUser(String userId) async {
    try {
      await _apiClient.dio.post(
        ApiEndpoints.blockUser(userId),
      );
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw ServerException.fromDio(e);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> updateFcmToken(UpdateFcmTokenModel request) async {
    try {
      await _apiClient.dio.put(
        ApiEndpoints.updateFcmToken,
        data: request.toJson(),
      );
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw ServerException.fromDio(e);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> updateProfileVisibility(UpdateVisibilityModel request) async {
    try {
      await _apiClient.dio.put(
        ApiEndpoints.updateVisibility,
        data: request.toJson(),
      );
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw ServerException.fromDio(e);
    }
  }
}

import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/gym/data/models/gym_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/data/models/badge_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/domain/entities/user.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/domain/enums/profile_visibility.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/domain/enums/user_role.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/domain/enums/user_status.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/verification/domain/enums/verification_status_enum.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/verification/domain/enums/verified_gender_enum.dart';

part 'user_model.freezed.dart';
part 'user_model.g.dart';

@freezed
class UserModel with _$UserModel {
  const UserModel._();

  @JsonSerializable(fieldRename: FieldRename.none, explicitToJson: true) // <-- ADD THIS
  const factory UserModel({
    required String userId,
    required String firstName,
    String? profilePhotoUrl,
    required VerificationStatus verificationStatus,
    required VerifiedGender verifiedGender,
    required ProfileVisibility profileVisibility,
    required UserRole role,
    required UserStatus status,
    required int groupSessionCount,
    required int noShowCount,
    GymModel? primaryGym,
    @Default([]) List<BadgeModel> userBadges,
  }) = _UserModel;

  factory UserModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) =>
      _$UserModelFromJson(json);

  User toEntity() {
    return User(
      userId: userId,
      firstName: firstName,
      profilePhotoUrl: profilePhotoUrl,
      verificationStatus: verificationStatus,
      verifiedGender: verifiedGender,
      profileVisibility: profileVisibility,
      role: role,
      status: status,
      groupSessionCount: groupSessionCount,
      noShowCount: noShowCount,
      primaryGym: primaryGym?.toEntity(),
      userBadges: userBadges.map((badge) => badge.toEntity()).toList(),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/exceptions.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/failure.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/data/datasources/user_remote_datasource.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/data/models/update_fcm_token_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/data/models/update_visibility_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/domain/entities/user.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/domain/enums/profile_visibility.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/domain/repositories/user_repository.dart';

@Injectable(as: UserRepository)
class UserRepositoryImpl implements UserRepository {
  final UserRemoteDatasource _remoteDatasource;

  UserRepositoryImpl(this._remoteDatasource);

  @override
  Future<User> getMyProfile() async {
    try {
      final userModel = await _remoteDatasource.getMyProfile();
      return userModel.toEntity();
    } on ServerException catch (e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<User> getUserProfile(String userId) async {
    try {
      final userModel = await _remoteDatasource.getUserProfile(userId);
      return userModel.toEntity();
    } on ServerException catch (e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> blockUser(String userId) async {
    try {
      await _remoteDatasource.blockUser(userId);
    } on ServerException catch (e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> updateFcmToken(String token) async {
    try {
      final requestModel = UpdateFcmTokenModel(fcmToken: token);
      await _remoteDatasource.updateFcmToken(requestModel);
    } on ServerException catch (e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> updateProfileVisibility(ProfileVisibility visibility) async {
    try {
      final requestModel = UpdateVisibilityModel(visibility: visibility);
      await _remoteDatasource.updateProfileVisibility(requestModel);
    } on ServerException catch (e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message);
    }
  }
}

import 'package:bloc/bloc.dart';
import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/failure.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/usecases/usecase.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/domain/entities/user.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/domain/enums/profile_visibility.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/domain/usecases/get_my_profile.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/domain/usecases/update_profile_visibility.dart';

part 'profile_bloc.freezed.dart';
part 'profile_event.dart';
part 'profile_state.dart';

@injectable
class ProfileBloc extends Bloc<ProfileEvent, ProfileState> {
  final GetMyProfile _getMyProfile;
  final UpdateProfileVisibility _updateProfileVisibility;

  ProfileBloc(
    this._getMyProfile,
    this._updateProfileVisibility,
  ) : super(const ProfileState.initial()) {
    on<_LoadMyProfile>(_onLoadMyProfile);
    on<_VisibilityChanged>(_onVisibilityChanged);
  }

  /// Handles fetching the current user's profile.
  Future<void> _onLoadMyProfile(
    _LoadMyProfile event,
    Emitter<ProfileState> emit,
  ) async {
    emit(const ProfileState.loading());
    try {
      final user = await _getMyProfile(NoParams());
      emit(ProfileState.loaded(user: user));
    } on ServerFailure catch (e) {
      emit(ProfileState.error(e.message));
    } catch (e) {
      emit(ProfileState.error(e.toString()));
    }
  }

  /// Handles updating the user's profile visibility.
  Future<void> _onVisibilityChanged(
    _VisibilityChanged event,
    Emitter<ProfileState> emit,
  ) async {
    // You might want to show a more subtle loading,
    // e.g., by adding a bool to the 'Loaded' state:
    // emit(ProfileState.loaded(user: currentUser, isUpdating: true));
    emit(const ProfileState.loading());

    try {
      final params = UpdateProfileVisibilityParams(
        visibility: event.newVisibility,
      );
      await _updateProfileVisibility(params);

      // On success, dispatch an event to reload the profile
      // to get the updated user object.
      add(const ProfileEvent.loadMyProfile());
    } on ServerFailure catch (e) {
      emit(ProfileState.error(e.message));
    } catch (e) {
      emit(ProfileState.error(e.toString()));
    }
  }
}

      return loaded(user);

  }

@freezed
class ProfileState with _$ProfileState {
  /// The initial state, before the profile has been loaded.
  const factory ProfileState.initial() = _Initial;

  /// The state while the profile is being fetched or updated.
  const factory ProfileState.loading() = _Loading;

  /// The state when the user's profile has been successfully loaded.
  const factory ProfileState.loaded({required User user}) = _Loaded;

  /// The state when an error occurred while fetching or updating.
  const factory ProfileState.error(String message) = _Error;
}
